# CLI reference

Generated from `HELP_ENTRIES` in `src/cli/help.ts` (the `phm commands` catalog) and `FLEET_SUBCOMMAND_LOADERS`, using the metadata reader behind `phm fleet verbs`. Do not edit by hand.

From the repository root, run `phm docs cli` to regenerate or `phm docs cli --check` to check without writing. Add `--json` for a structured report.
From source: `npm run dev -- docs cli`. The existing `node --import tsx scripts/generate-cli-reference.ts` entry point uses the same generator.

Each row preserves one registered usage and its summary. Flags come from that usage. JSON examples use the same notation: replace `<placeholders>`, choose `a|b` alternatives, and omit optional `[arguments]` as needed. Examples are not executed.
Missing fleet summaries are marked explicitly; flags hidden behind `[opts]` or unavailable usage metadata require `phm <command> --help`. This describes the checkout; an installed older binary may differ.

| Command | Summary | Flags | JSON example |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>phm admission explain [--json]</code> | Read existing admission inputs, the adaptive launch interval and its reason. Advisory; launches nothing. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm admission explain --json</code> |
| <code>phm agentnet peers list&#124;read&#124;send &#124; channels propose &#124; identity register [--json]</code> | Session-bound team peer messages, verified inbox and Slack provisioning drafts. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agentnet peers list&#124;read&#124;send &#124; channels propose &#124; identity register --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents bus channels list&#124;test &lt;id&gt; [--json]</code> | List registered channel adapters and probe the local bus or handoff store. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents bus channels list&#124;test &lt;id&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents bus contract [--json]</code> | Print the Agent Bus v2 contract: schema version, providers, body kinds, work statuses, claim key forms. Read-only. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents bus contract --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents bus dm&#124;channel&#124;handoff ... [--json]</code> | Unified agent bus: DMs, member-only channels and handoffs on the one local bus store, with priority wake. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents bus dm&#124;channel&#124;handoff ... --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents bus id register&#124;list [&lt;handle&gt;] [--json]</code> | Stable agent identity registry: one agt_ id per handle across providers and models. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents bus id register&#124;list [&lt;handle&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents bus inbox&#124;wakes&#124;adapters [&lt;agent&gt;] [--json]</code> | Read an identity inbox or wake hints, or list the claude/codex/devin/cursor/grok session adapters. Read-only. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents bus inbox&#124;wakes&#124;adapters [&lt;agent&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents mesh status [--json]</code> | Flat agent mesh: direct addresses, lanes, last-seen and current tasks. Read-only. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents mesh status --json</code> |
| <code>phm agents roster [--json]</code> | Unified live session roster. One probe per source, about 5s each, unknown on timeout or an unverified command. Read-only. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm agents roster --json</code> |
| <code>phm amplify &lt;status&#124;tick&#124;goal&gt; [--json]</code> | Objective, expiring capacity, vitals and leader decisions; tick journals advisory actions only. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm amplify &lt;status&#124;tick&#124;goal&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm app restart --json</code> | Report a held restart while the native UI owns live runtime state; does not quit or update the app. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm app restart --json</code> |
| <code>phm app steer --text &lt;msg&gt; [--json]</code> | Send one message to the leader agents through the shared inbox receiver. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--text</code> | <code>phm app steer --text &lt;msg&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm app update status [--json]</code> | Inspect local desktop update status without downloading or installing. Read-only: reports current stage, available version, deferred reason, running job counts. Exit 0 success, 2 bad usage. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm app update status --json</code> |
| <code>phm app update status [--json]</code> | Read the local signed desktop-update stage and deferral counts; never downloads or installs. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm app update status --json</code> |
| <code>phm approvals [--team &lt;id&gt;] [--json]</code> | What needs a human: decisions only Mason can make first, then pending team approvals, the Security pin queue (read-only) and explicit holds; same projection as the desktop Approvals panel. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--team</code> | <code>phm approvals [--team &lt;id&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm approvals answer &lt;ref&gt; &lt;option&gt; [--json]</code> | Mason only (agent processes are refused): record his answer once, with who answered, the channel and the time. It only records; nothing is paid or sent. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm approvals answer &lt;ref&gt; &lt;option&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm approvals ask --question &lt;text&gt; --options yes,no [--expires &lt;iso&gt;] [--by &lt;name&gt;] [--json]</code> | For agents: raise one decision only Mason can make, with its own options; it is numbered and an identical open one is reused. | <code>--by</code>, <code>--expires</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--options</code>, <code>--question</code> | <code>phm approvals ask --question &lt;text&gt; --options yes,no [--expires &lt;iso&gt;] [--by &lt;name&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm apps list&#124;open&#124;quit&#124;restart&#124;focus&#124;status [&lt;app&gt;] [--json] [--dry-run] &#124; apps request open&#124;quit&#124;focus &lt;app&gt; [--json]</code> | Manage desktop apps named in ~/.ashlr/apps-allow.json (empty by default). Per-app action policy; logged to the feed. Requests and unsafe quits go to needs-you. | <code>--dry-run</code>, <code>--json</code> | <code>phm apps list&#124;open&#124;quit&#124;restart&#124;focus&#124;status [&lt;app&gt;] --json [--dry-run] &#124; apps request open&#124;quit&#124;focus &lt;app&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm apps log [--limit &lt;n&gt;] [--json]</code> | Every app open, focus, quit and restart on record in the feed, newest first, with its outcome. Read-only. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--limit</code> | <code>phm apps log [--limit &lt;n&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm ask "&lt;question&gt;"</code> | Local RAG across the indexed portfolio; cites repo/file:line. --allow-cloud opt-in. &#96;ask status&#96; prints the ask.status.v1 stream from the metrics jobs ledger. | - | - |
| <code>phm asks [--json] &#124; asks answer &lt;id&gt; &lt;reply&gt;</code> | What only Mason can do (password, 2FA, device, payment, outreach, a usage percent, a credit approval) and a one-word or one-number answer for each. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm asks --json &#124; asks answer &lt;id&gt; &lt;reply&gt;</code> |
| <code>phm audit [N] [--team &lt;id&gt;] [--json] [--action &lt;verb&gt;] [--result &lt;r&gt;] [--since &lt;when&gt;]</code> | Tail the append-only audit trail; --team lists workspace actions (read-only). | <code>--action</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--result</code>, <code>--since</code>, <code>--team</code> | <code>phm audit [N] [--team &lt;id&gt;] --json [--action &lt;verb&gt;] [--result &lt;r&gt;] [--since &lt;when&gt;]</code> |
| <code>phm authority grant&#124;re-approve [--yes]</code> | Sign a standing grant (or its continuation) with Touch ID via ashlr-custody and install it; fail-closed while compiled trust roots are empty. | <code>--yes</code> | - |
| <code>phm authority ledger verify&#124;tail</code> | Verify or read the hash-chained authority ledger (~/.ashlr/authority/ledger.jsonl). | - | - |
| <code>phm authority protect --print&#124;--apply</code> | Print (or apply after confirmation) the GitHub rulesets for the grant's server-enforced repos. | <code>--apply</code>, <code>--print</code> | - |
| <code>phm authority revoke [--reason &lt;text&gt;]</code> | Engage Stop, switch autonomy off and require a new signed grant (Touch ID) to resume. | <code>--reason</code> | - |
| <code>phm authority setup [--dry-run [--json]]</code> | Guided Phase 0: custody key, trust-root PR, GitHub App, Claude token, canary, rulesets, first grant, switch, daemon service - asks before every step; --dry-run --json prints the checklist as JSON. | <code>--dry-run</code>, <code>--json</code> | <code>phm authority setup [--dry-run --json]</code> |
| <code>phm authority status [--json]</code> | Standing authority: grant state, autonomy switch, Stop, rollout stage, ledger chain and custody (read-only). | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm authority status --json</code> |
| <code>phm authority stop&#124;clear-stop</code> | Engage Stop (~/.ashlr/KILL: halts running agents, cancels armed merges) or clear it; stopping never asks, clearing is ledgered first. | - | - |
| <code>phm authority switch &lt;off&#124;propose&#124;autonomous&gt;</code> | Lower autonomy instantly; raise it only up to what the signed grant allows (past it needs Touch ID). | - | - |
| <code>phm autofix [--apply] [--dry-run] [--json] [--only &lt;fixer&gt;]</code> | Act on Eng Ops incident files (prefix a, b, d, e; g is detect-only). Dry run unless --apply. Refuses when ~/.ashlr/KILL exists. Never reads the incident body into a command. See docs/AUTOFIX.md. | <code>--apply</code>, <code>--dry-run</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--only</code> | <code>phm autofix [--apply] [--dry-run] --json [--only &lt;fixer&gt;]</code> |
| <code>phm automations fire &lt;id&gt; [--dry-run]</code> | Check one automation's trigger now and dispatch what it finds through the lane's own gates (grant, KILL, budgets); --dry-run shows what would happen and writes nothing. | <code>--dry-run</code> | - |
| <code>phm automations list&#124;templates&#124;add&#124;enable&#124;disable&#124;remove</code> | Automations: labelled issues, a red default branch, RRULE schedules, a local webhook or Telegram /task become tasks for the fleet, cloud, Devin or review - with queue, per-day, concurrency and spend limits. | - | - |
| <code>phm autonomy [--hours 24] [--json]</code> | Measure merges, human involvement, fleet improvements, releases and main health from local evidence. | <code>--hours</code>, <code>--json</code> | <code>phm autonomy [--hours 24] --json</code> |
| <code>phm autopilot &lt;pr&gt; [--apply] [--watch]</code> | Drive one of Phantom's own PRs to green: fix red CI and review feedback with an agent, merge a conflicting or behind base, re-run infra failures once, re-verify; stops and says why when a failure is not the PR's. Plan only unless --apply; never force-pushes or merges the PR. | <code>--apply</code>, <code>--watch</code> | - |
| <code>phm backlog --json</code> | Emit raw JSON backlog. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm backlog --json</code> |
| <code>phm backlog --limit &lt;n&gt;</code> | Show only the top N items. | <code>--limit</code> | - |
| <code>phm backlog --repo &lt;path&gt;</code> | Filter backlog to a specific enrolled repo. | <code>--repo</code> | - |
| <code>phm backlog --source &lt;src&gt;</code> | Filter backlog by source: issue&#124;todo&#124;test&#124;dep&#124;doc&#124;security&#124;plugin&#124;self&#124;lint&#124;goal&#124;hygiene&#124;invent. | <code>--source</code> | - |
| <code>phm backlog refresh</code> | Re-scan all enrolled repos and rebuild the backlog. | - | - |
| <code>phm backlog</code> | Scored work queue across enrolled repos (issues, TODOs, tests, deps, docs, security, plugins, goals, hygiene, invention). | - | - |
| <code>phm benchmark run&#124;--compare-reports</code> | Explicit local-agent benchmark trials, or offline matched-receipt comparison; real checkers and private traces. | <code>--compare-reports</code> | - |
| <code>phm best-of-n [--repo --title -n]</code> | Generate N candidate diffs (multi-model), critic-select, file the winner as a proposal (M142/M333). | <code>--repo</code>, <code>--title</code>, <code>-n</code> | - |
| <code>phm bets check "&lt;goal text&gt;" [--json]</code> | Before queuing work: does it already exist? Looks the goal up in CLI help, API routes, web-ui panes and docs headings; says new, partial or exists with up to 10 matches and one suggested delta. Read-only, offline. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm bets check "&lt;goal text&gt;" --json</code> |
| <code>phm bets check --from-prompt &lt;file&gt; [--gate] [--json]</code> | Step 0 for a job: reads the task from a queue prompt file; --gate exits 0 new, 2 partial, 3 exists, 1 error. Read-only, offline. | <code>--from-prompt</code>, <code>--gate</code>, <code>--json</code> | <code>phm bets check --from-prompt &lt;file&gt; [--gate] --json</code> |
| <code>phm bets list&#124;launch&#124;score&#124;kill [--json]</code> | Bets lane ledger: launch scored experiments, run the kill-fast scorer, kill by hand. Append-only metrics/bets.jsonl. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm bets list&#124;launch&#124;score&#124;kill --json</code> |
| <code>phm bets propose [--lane &lt;lane&gt;] [--count &lt;n&gt;] [--json]</code> | A lane proposes its own next work: gathers TODO/FIXME comments, open roadmap items, skipped tests and undocumented commands, drops what bets check says exists, ranks user-facing then smallest first; each row has an id, repo, size, goal and Acceptance line. Read-only, writes no queue file. | <code>--count</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--lane</code> | <code>phm bets propose [--lane &lt;lane&gt;] [--count &lt;n&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm bets suggest [--json] [--limit N]</code> | Rank the next jobs worth queuing from idle lanes, expiring usage, red mains and unreleased repos, each with a queue line to copy; bets explain &lt;id&gt; shows the scoring. Read-only. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--limit</code> | <code>phm bets suggest --json [--limit N]</code> |
| <code>phm bots list&#124;show&#124;create --dry-run&#124;explain&#124;seats&#124;handoff --seat &lt;id&gt; [--apply] [--json]</code> | Provider-neutral bot metadata, seat quota and draining handoffs. Handoff is dry-run unless --apply. | <code>--apply</code>, <code>--dry-run</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--seat</code> | <code>phm bots list&#124;show&#124;create --dry-run&#124;explain&#124;seats&#124;handoff --seat &lt;id&gt; [--apply] --json</code> |
| <code>phm browser [tabs&#124;doctor&#124;open &lt;url&gt;&#124;shot [&lt;path.png&gt;]&#124;close] [--json]</code> | Drive Safari, Phantom's own agent browser: list agent tabs, open a web address, save a screenshot, close its windows. doctor says whether remote automation is on and the one step to enable it. Chrome is always refused. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm browser [tabs&#124;doctor&#124;open &lt;url&gt;&#124;shot [&lt;path.png&gt;]&#124;close] --json</code> |
| <code>phm budget &lt;show&#124;add&gt; [--json]</code> | Per-pool remaining budget: show the ledger, or record a manual reading or parsed CLI/log output. Manual readings override automatic ones until their reset; raw output is never stored. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm budget &lt;show&#124;add&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm budget get [--json]</code> | Read the owner spend budget in USD. A missing provider is 0 for the month. This does not change the budget. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm budget get --json</code> |
| <code>phm budget set --provider &lt;id&gt; --period day&#124;week&#124;month --usd &lt;n&gt; [--json]</code> | Owner-only spend budget change from an interactive terminal, with one audit line. An amount above 0 stays ineffective until a Security pin-chain or owner signature root exists. Agents and non-TTY calls are refused. The committed default stays 0. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--period</code>, <code>--provider</code>, <code>--usd</code> | <code>phm budget set --provider &lt;id&gt; --period day&#124;week&#124;month --usd &lt;n&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm bus send&#124;inbox&#124;ack [--json]</code> | Durable local message mailbox: send JSON, read a recipient inbox and acknowledge messages; separate from the task board. &#96;phm bus send --help&#96; for options. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm bus send&#124;inbox&#124;ack --json</code> |
| <code>phm bus thread&#124;task &lt;verb&gt; [--peer &lt;id&gt;] [--json]</code> | Shared task board: local threads plus task claim, handoff, renew and release tied to fenced fleet queue claims. Cooperative labels only; no execution authority. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--peer</code> | <code>phm bus thread&#124;task &lt;verb&gt; [--peer &lt;id&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm capacity publish [--json]</code> | Refresh local subscription usage metadata once with this node’s local authentication. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm capacity publish --json</code> |
| <code>phm caps list&#124;validate&#124;generate [--registry &lt;path&gt;] [--out &lt;dir&gt;] [--pin-stage] [--check] [--json]</code> | Capability registry: surfaces, models, hosting edges, account references and capabilities; validate it and generate pinned per-agent adapters to staging (never into live agent config dirs). See docs/CAPABILITY-REGISTRY.md. | <code>--check</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--out</code>, <code>--pin-stage</code>, <code>--registry</code> | <code>phm caps list&#124;validate&#124;generate [--registry &lt;path&gt;] [--out &lt;dir&gt;] [--pin-stage] [--check] --json</code> |
| <code>phm chain verify [--json]</code> | Synthetic in-memory chain checks. Does not verify live transports. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm chain verify --json</code> |
| <code>phm channels add &lt;kind&gt; &#124; status [--json] &#124; slack status&#124;map [--json]</code> | Bring a channel up (config only, credentials stay in custody), read the per-channel rollup, or inspect the Slack bridge mapping offline. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm channels add &lt;kind&gt; &#124; status --json &#124; slack status&#124;map --json</code> |
| <code>phm check [--base &lt;ref&gt;] [--json]</code> | Run incremental typechecks, cached lint and affected tests from a Phantom checkout; development feedback only. | <code>--base</code>, <code>--json</code> | <code>phm check [--base &lt;ref&gt;] --json</code> |
| <code>phm checkpoint resume &lt;file&gt; [--json]</code> | Validate and print a structured agent checkpoint for a fresh session. See docs/AGENT-CHECKPOINT.md. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm checkpoint resume &lt;file&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm checkpoint save &lt;source&gt; &lt;destination&gt; &lt;worktree&gt; [--json]</code> | Save a handoff with a Git worktree inventory. See docs/AGENT-CHECKPOINT.md. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm checkpoint save &lt;source&gt; &lt;destination&gt; &lt;worktree&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm ci baseline [--repo owner/repo] [--json] [--queue] [--queue-file /absolute/path] [--apply]</code> | Analyze shared CI baselines; plan master fixes and post-merge branch updates. | <code>--apply</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--queue</code>, <code>--queue-file</code>, <code>--repo</code> | <code>phm ci baseline [--repo owner/repo] --json [--queue] [--queue-file /absolute/path] [--apply]</code> |
| <code>phm ci reruns [--json]</code> | Read resident CI rerun journal and hourly counts. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm ci reruns --json</code> |
| <code>phm ci-metrics [--json]</code> | Per-repo daily turn-time (red to green) metrics; writes ~/.ashlr/ci-metrics.json. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm ci-metrics --json</code> |
| <code>phm claim acquire &lt;work-key&gt; --lane &lt;lane&gt; [--json]</code> | Claim a work key before starting. Refuses a live claim or open pull on that key and returns the holder. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--lane</code> | <code>phm claim acquire &lt;work-key&gt; --lane &lt;lane&gt; --json</code> |
| <code>phm claim release &lt;work-key&gt; --lane &lt;lane&gt; --reason open&#124;merge&#124;abandon [--json]</code> | Release this lane claim when a pull opens, merges, or is abandoned. | <code>--json</code>, <code>--lane</code>, <code>--reason</code> | <code>phm claim release &lt;work-key&gt; --lane &lt;lane&gt; --reason open&#124;merge&#124;abandon --json</code> |
| <code>phm cloud launch "&lt;task&gt;" [--repo o/n] [--base b]</code> | Start a Claude Code cloud session that delivers a draft PR (spends Claude credits; never merges). Repo defaults to this folder's GitHub origin. | <code>--base</code>, <code>--repo</code> | - |
| <code>phm cloud list&#124;refresh&#124;improve&#124;budget&#124;backlog</code> | Cloud tasks and their PRs, a GitHub refresh, self-improvement launches (--count N), the credit budget (an estimate; flags update it) and the backlog. | - | - |
| <code>phm commands [--json]</code> | List every command: what it does, its arguments and whether it changes state. The desktop palette (Cmd+K) shows the same list. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm commands --json</code> |
| <code>phm comms &lt;status&#124;thread&#124;send-owner&#124;cycle&#124;digest&gt;</code> | Operator comms. status --json, thread and send-owner are the one owner thread; replies go only to the configured owner. KILL halts adapters. | - | - |
| <code>phm comms-agent status&#124;run&#124;drafts [--json]</code> | Comms role under the Leader. Triage archives bulk and bot mail and drafts replies. Sends need an owner standing approval. No lane is hard-coded. See docs/COMMS-AGENT.md. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm comms-agent status&#124;run&#124;drafts --json</code> |
| <code>phm companions [--json] [--root &lt;dir&gt;] [--bin-dir &lt;dir&gt;]</code> | Read-only Secrets/Locus/Lexicon executable identity and version inventory; no runtime readiness or activation. | <code>--bin-dir</code>, <code>--json</code>, <code>--root</code> | <code>phm companions --json [--root &lt;dir&gt;] [--bin-dir &lt;dir&gt;]</code> |
| <code>phm completions zsh&#124;bash</code> | Print a shell completion script to stdout. | - | - |
| <code>phm conductor-permit request&#124;mint&#124;inspect&#124;stage</code> | Dormant signed one-shot goal-conductor permit operator; fail-closed while source trust roots are empty. | - | - |
| <code>phm config [list&#124;get &lt;k&gt;&#124;set &lt;k&gt; &lt;v&gt;]</code> | Read/write a config value. &#96;list&#96; prints a summary; no args prints the path. | - | - |
| <code>phm config effective [--json]</code> | Read-only effective autonomy/daemon/foundry/backend settings. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm config effective --json</code> |
| <code>phm config path</code> | Print the path to config.json. | - | - |
| <code>phm config set &lt;k&gt; --json &lt;v&gt;</code> | Set a structured (array/object) config value as JSON. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm config set &lt;k&gt; --json &lt;v&gt;</code> |
| <code>phm connectors add&#124;list&#124;remove&#124;show&#124;import&#124;exec&#124;config [--json]</code> | Manage credential-free connector profiles, launch connectors with phantom-injected credentials, and print client configs that hold no token. | <code>--json</code> | <code>phm connectors add&#124;list&#124;remove&#124;show&#124;import&#124;exec&#124;config --json</code> |
| <code>phm console [--port N] [--no-open]</code> | Open the Phantom console at /app/ for connected coding resources. Runs use account readiness and authority; prints read + mutation tokens. | <code>--no-open</code>, <code>--port</code> | - |
| <code>phm constitution check [--root &lt;repo&gt;] [--json] [paths...]</code> | Check constitution rule definitions and references in prompts and templates (read-only). | <code>--json</code>, <code>--root</code> | <code>phm constitution check [--root &lt;repo&gt;] --json [paths...]</code> |

Also says who owns the load (fleet jobs, Claude loop, Devin cloud, local model, main guardian, other), the admission guard state and any job idle over 45 minutes that still uses CPU. ## Fleet, leader and host verbs without help text

These registered verbs have no one-line help in their source yet. Their rows above say so; the gap is shown, not guessed.
